# Case Compendium - Support & Regulatory Archive

This archive collects procedural records, correspondence and rulings.
Lines are wrapped at the source; every newline should render as a break.
It is intentionally long so the document view has to virtualize it.

## Case 1: EXT-CASE-500137

**Filed:** 2025-02-15  
**Registry:** Continental Freight GmbH  
**Jurisdiction:** Rotterdam

### Summary

The parties exchanged submissions over several weeks and the tribunal reviewed the technical record in full.
Correspondence was logged as it arrived and each attachment was verified against the manifest.
Where a discrepancy was found the submitting party was asked to re-file within five working days.
No adverse inference was drawn from the initial formatting issues, which were treated as clerical.

The claimant, Johan Rossi, alleged losses of EUR 252,228.00 arising from the events described below.
The respondent, Continental Freight GmbH, denied liability and relied on GDPR Art. 6(1)(f).

### Parties

- Claimant: Johan Rossi, represented by Lena Tanaka
- Respondent: Continental Freight GmbH
- Presiding: Tomas Tanaka

### Statutory basis

The tribunal considered D.Lgs. 231/2001 alongside Art. 1218 c.c..
Damages, if any, were to be assessed at EUR 252,228.00.

> On the question of foreseeability the tribunal observed that
> the risk was known to Continental Freight GmbH well before the filing date.

### Procedural log

1. 2025-07-01 - filing received
2. 2021-09-04 - hearing scheduled
3. 2022-06-22 - evidence exchanged

4. 2024-11-09 - oral argument
5. 2023-07-27 - decision rendered

## Case 2: EXT-CASE-500274

**Filed:** 2020-11-03  
**Registry:** Orion Municipal Court  
**Jurisdiction:** Porto

### Summary

The parties exchanged submissions over several weeks and the tribunal reviewed the technical record in full.
Correspondence was logged as it arrived and each attachment was verified against the manifest.
Where a discrepancy was found the submitting party was asked to re-file within five working days.
No adverse inference was drawn from the initial formatting issues, which were treated as clerical.

The claimant, Johan Meyer, alleged losses of EUR 91,604.00 arising from the events described below.
The respondent, Orion Municipal Court, denied liability and relied on Art. 96 c.p.c..

### Parties

- Claimant: Johan Meyer, represented by Yuki Nguyen
- Respondent: Orion Municipal Court
- Presiding: Chen Rossi

### Statutory basis

The tribunal considered Art. 2043 c.c. alongside Art. 96 c.p.c..
Damages, if any, were to be assessed at EUR 91,604.00.

> On the question of foreseeability the tribunal observed that
> the risk was known to Orion Municipal Court well before the filing date.

### Procedural log

1. 2022-02-04 - filing received
2. 2020-09-05 - hearing scheduled
3. 2022-08-12 - evidence exchanged

4. 2024-12-23 - oral argument
5. 2023-07-19 - decision rendered
